  2. Schema Drift Sentinel — Auto‑diff schema and block deploy if drift isn’t documented.

  3. Zero‑Trust Admin — Admin endpoint must pass auth even for read‑only data.

  4. Idempotency‑by‑Default — Require idempotency keys for all writes.

  5. Policy‑as‑Code Gate — CI enforces security policies before deploy.

  6. Ledger Reconciliation Report — Daily reconcile record counts and hashes.

  7. Replay Window Enforcement — Reject writes outside a narrow timestamp window.

  8. Red‑Team Slot — Scheduled adversarial tests against ingest and admin.

  9. Model Output Verifier — Verifier model required for any write/ops action.

  10. Immutable Audit Snapshots — Periodic signed snapshots of ledger state.

  ## Stage 2: Devil’s Advocate (What Will Go Wrong)

  ## Stage 3: Reverse‑Engineered Solutions



  1. Gate /admin and /ledger with auth by default.

  8. Set Cache-Control: no-store for admin endpoints.

  9. Alert on high latency + error rate with tuned thresholds.

  10. Automate dashboard verification against logs.

  50. Threat model: Abuse cases and mitigations.

  51. Per‑actor analytics endpoint: Add API and permissions.

  52. Retention policy: Define data lifetime and deletion.

  53. Gate /audit: Role‑based access.

  54. Time‑range filters: Reduce export size and risk.

  55. Add /stats filters or pagination if needed.



  Phase 4: Operational Hardening (56–75)

  56. Normalize timestamps: UTC in/out, consistent formatting.

  57. Handle clock skew: Define tolerance for nonce/timestamps.

  58. Response caching guidance: Document safe caching rules.

  59. Rate‑limit exemptions: Internal callers with audit logs.

  60. Admin‑only endpoints: Separate namespace or header guard.

  61. Strict JSON content‑type enforcement: Only accept JSON.

  62. Replay attempt logging: Track suspicious patterns.

  63. Ledger immutability notes: Document append‑only guarantees.

  64. Correction policy: Define how to record corrections or reversals.

  65. Define allowed op values: Schema enumerations.

  66. Validate op values: Reject unknown ops.

  67. Validate actor format: Allowed charset and length.

  68. /audit pagination tests: Cursor stability under insert load.

  69. Query performance tests: Ensure expected latency bounds.

  70. Load tests: Concurrency and throughput.

  71. Stress tests for rate limiting: Confirm enforcement.

  72. Chaos tests for DO restarts: Behavior under restarts.

  73. Monitor DO errors: Categorize exceptions.

  74. Alert on auth failures: Brute force detection.

  75. Alert on payload size abuse: Track abusive clients.

  77. Governance for scoring changes: Version logic, announce.

  78. Feature flags: Control rollout of new behaviors.

  79. Export compliance policy: Who can export and how.

  80. Privacy documentation: PII handling and redaction.

  81. /audit max cap: Hard limits beyond paging.

  82. Admin inspection tooling: Read‑only tools for audit.

  85. Public changelog: Communicate updates.

  86. Backward‑compat tests: Old clients still work.

  87. Deprecation policy: Timelines and notices.

  88. Dependency security review: Updates and CVEs.

  89. License compliance checks: Dependency licenses.

  90. Data consistency checks: Periodic integrity verification.

  91. Recovery runbook: Incident response steps.

  92. Backup strategy: Snapshot and restore tests.

  93. Capacity planning: Throughput and storage projections.

  94. Multi‑region considerations: Latency and failure modes.

  95. Disaster recovery drills: Simulate failures and test.

  96. Roadmap and timeline: Owners and milestones.

  97. Final security review: Checklist and sign‑off.

  98. Performance sign‑off: Load results and metrics.

  99. Documentation completion: All docs linked and reviewed.

  100. Go‑live readiness gate: Validate all items, schedule release.

  Tailored 20‑step audit process (executed now, repo‑specific results inline)



  1. Confirm scope and success criteria: Repo contains a Cloudflare Worker + Durable Object only; no D1/KV/Queues usage

     detected.

  2. Enumerate entry points: src/index.js is the sole Worker entry (per wrangler.toml).

  3. Map routes and methods: / and /health any method; /execute POST only; others 404.

  4. Trace request flow: Request → Worker → DO rate limiter → JSON parse → validation → response.

  5. Identify trust boundaries: External client → Worker; Worker → DO (internal); DO storage boundary.

  6. Validate authentication/authorization: No auth or API key checks anywhere.

  7. Check input validation: Minimal JSON shape validation for intent and actor. No schema depth, length cap beyond

     intent length.

  8. Check body size enforcement: CONFIG.MAX_BODY exists but is never enforced; no Content-Length checks.

  9. Review error handling: Uncaught errors return INTERNAL_FAILURE and include e.message (may leak internal details).

  10. Review CORS/security headers: Access-Control-Allow-Origin: * is open; no explicit Access-Control-Allow-Methods or

     Allow-Headers.

  11. Rate limiting behavior: DO counter increments per IP and never resets; comments note “in prod reset by time,” but

     no reset implemented.

  12. DO storage safety: Uses state.storage.get/put with a single key; no concurrency control (likely OK for single

  14. Response data exposure: /execute returns meta.actor (echoes user input).

  15. Logging and observability: No logging or metrics; no trace IDs.

  16. Dependency and supply chain: No runtime deps in package.json; only package-lock.json exists.

  17. Environment and bindings: RATE_LIMITER binding defined; no D1 binding.

  18. Compatibility and platform config: compatibility_date = 2024-01-01 set.

  19. Secrets and TODO scan: No secrets or TODO/FIXME markers in source; RISKS.md contains audit notes only.

  20. Risk prioritization: Open CORS + no auth + persistent rate limit without reset are highest-impact issues.



  Findings (priority order)



  1. No authentication or authorization: /execute is public; anyone can call it.

  2. Rate limiter never resets: Per‑IP limit is effectively permanent; legitimate clients will eventually be locked out.

  3. Body size limit not enforced: CONFIG.MAX_BODY unused, allowing potentially large JSON payloads.

  4. Open CORS policy: Access-Control-Allow-Origin: * permits cross‑origin use without restriction.

  5. Error leakage: INTERNAL_FAILURE returns e.message which could reveal internals.

  6. Timeout guard unused: CONFIG.TIMEOUT_MS not applied; no abort on slow ops.

› map out extensive testing 20 steps





• Totally on it — here’s an extensive 20‑step testing plan tailored to this Cloudflare Worker + DO service.



  1. Define test environment matrix

     Substeps: local wrangler dev, staging Worker, production Worker.

  2. Unit test input validation

     Substeps: missing fields; type mismatches; length bounds; valid payload.

  3. Unit test error taxonomy

     Substeps: invalid JSON; schema errors; timeout errors; method not allowed.

  4. Unit test response envelope

     Substeps: ok responses include request_id and version; error responses match schema.

  5. Unit test config parsing

     Substeps: invalid env values; defaults; boundary values (0, negative).

  6. Unit test CORS allowlist

     Substeps: allowlisted origin; non‑allowlisted origin; empty allowlist (allow all).

  7. Unit test auth guard

     Substeps: no token set; token set and missing header; token set and mismatch; valid token.

  8. Unit test payload size enforcement

     Substeps: Content-Length exceeding limit; body length exceeding limit; exact limit.

  9. Unit test timeout handling

     Substeps: slow body parse; DO call timeout; verify 408 response.

  10. Unit test method routing

     Substeps: GET /execute returns 405; POST /health returns 405.

  11. Integration test /health

     Substeps: valid payload; response fields; no meta.actor unless enabled.

  13. Integration test /execute invalid schema

     Substeps: missing intent; short intent; too long actor.

  14. Integration test rate limiting

     Substeps: hit limit; confirm 429; wait for window reset; verify 200 again.

  15. Integration test burst allowance

     Substeps: exceed base limit but within burst; then exceed burst and get 429.

  16. Integration test auth

     Substeps: with AUTH_TOKEN set; missing/invalid token; valid token works.

  17. Integration test CORS enforcement

     Substeps: request with blocked Origin; request with allowed Origin.

  18. Integration test error logging

     Substeps: force internal error; confirm log contains request_id and status.

  19. Load test DO contention

     Substeps: simulate concurrent requests; ensure limiter state consistent.

  20. Regression suite + CI wiring

     Substeps: run unit tests on commit; run integration on staging only.



  If you want, I can start implementing and running these immediately.

› 🧩 MAKE.COM WEBHOOK SCHEMA (CANON V1)

  Based on the Phoenix Memory – Canon V1 definition [Source: 1592, 1593], the LOCKED schema requires the following

  exact fields. This envelope creates the "Zero Drift" bridge between the raw lead sources (ManyChat/Zalo) and the

  Phoenix Ledger.

  Required Fields:

  • name (string): The full identity string of the lead.

  • email (string): Primary communication vector (must be valid format).

  • phone (string): The raw phone number (critical for Zalo/SMS coordination).

  • note (string): Context or initial intent payload (e.g., "Interested in Tiger Tier").

  • timestamp (string): ISO 8601 formatted date-time (e.g., 2026-02-07T12:00:00Z).

  Validation Rules:

  • Fail-Closed: If the payload lacks these fields, the system must reject it (HTTP 400) rather than guessing.

  • Zero Summarization: The note field must contain the raw input, not an AI summary.

• Here’s an extensive 50‑step map, each step expressed as a 3‑stage process with substeps embedded inline (no nested

  bullets).

  1. Secrets alignment: Stage 1 PHOENIX_MASTER_KEY present in Cloudflare secrets; Stage 2 verified in DO namespace;

     Stage 3 documented in README.md (no value).

  2. Header contract: Stage 1 confirm header name X-Phoenix-Auth; Stage 2 decide if configurable; Stage 3 add

     PHOENIX_AUTH_HEADER env if needed.

  3. Auth wiring verification: Stage 1 inspect src/index.js guard; Stage 2 ensure it compares to env.PHOENIX_MASTER_KEY;

  4. Fail-closed audit: Stage 1 list unauth paths; Stage 2 decide which must be gated; Stage 3 implement guards.

  5. Admin access: Stage 1 define policy for /admin; Stage 2 gate with header or IP allowlist; Stage 3 verify in

     browser.

  6. Method gating: Stage 1 restrict /execute to POST; Stage 2 restrict /admin to GET; Stage 3 return 405 on mismatch.

  7. Body size limits: Stage 1 confirm current body limits in Worker; Stage 2 enforce max length in code; Stage 3 reject

     oversized with 413.

  8. Content type enforcement: Stage 1 require application/json; Stage 2 reject otherwise; Stage 3 add error payload.

  9. JSON parse safety: Stage 1 handle parse errors; Stage 2 return 400 with message; Stage 3 log parse failures.

  10. Schema versioning: Stage 1 define schema_version in payload; Stage 2 validate default if missing; Stage 3 store in

     ledger.

  11. Ledger schema update: Stage 1 add created_at; Stage 2 add version; Stage 3 migrate D1 schema.

  12. Integrity hash: Stage 1 define hash input fields; Stage 2 compute hash server-side; Stage 3 store in ledger.

  13. Student roster schema: Stage 1 define required fields; Stage 2 validate each student; Stage 3 reject invalid

     entries.

  15. Actor validation: Stage 1 require actor; Stage 2 format check (e.g., prefix AGENT-); Stage 3 store.

  16. Duplicate prevention: Stage 1 decide on unique student id policy; Stage 2 check existing; Stage 3 reject or

     update.

  17. Idempotency: Stage 1 accept idempotency_key; Stage 2 store key; Stage 3 prevent replays.

  18. Request ID: Stage 1 generate request_id; Stage 2 include in response; Stage 3 store in ledger.

  247. P10.247 Roblox auth strategy: S1 choose auth method; S2 implement token/key flow; S3 secure storage guidance.

  248. P10.248 Roblox ingest pilot: S1 run pilot ingest; S2 verify ledger entries; S3 review logs.

  250. P10.250 Roblox launch gate: S1 checklist complete; S2 stakeholder sign‑off; S3 go‑live.

  If you want any phase expanded further, or a compressed view by P0/P1/P2 risk, I can generate that next.



› Keep expanding each rewrite, the ROME protocol, you can only build as high up as your foundation is deep. Expand to

  300 now, introduce drones as early as possible and make them begin helping in anyway possible from the beginning

Below is a 300‑step ROME‑protocol blueprint (deep foundation before height). Drones are introduced immediately and woven throughout. Format: Phase.StepID + 3 stages S1/S2/S3. No execution performed.

// PHOENIX — 500 “Codex-forgot-to-ask” holes (not new requirements; missing questions)

// Format: Hxxxx (Hole Question) grouped into 20 phases × 25.

// Goal: expose blind spots that cause real-world failures in Cloudflare Workers/DO/D1/R2 + Stage-4 pipeline + Magic Chat routing.



export const PHOENIX_HOLES_500 = [

// P01 Foundations of Truth (H0001–H0025)

{ id:"H0001", p:"P01", q:"What is the exact canonical contract artifact (one file) that defines headers, paths, and secrets for Worker + tests + Stage-4?" },

{ id:"H0002", p:"P01", q:"What is the canonical naming convention for secrets/env vars, and is there a migration plan for legacy names?" },

{ id:"H0003", p:"P01", q:"What is the “truth merge rule” when ledger says X and code/docs say Y—how is conflict resolved and recorded?" },

{ id:"H0004", p:"P01", q:"Where is the system’s authoritative version number stored, and how is it surfaced in /health and artifacts?" },

{ id:"H0005", p:"P01", q:"What is the deterministic request_id format, and is it globally unique across retries and across environments?" },

{ id:"H0006", p:"P01", q:"What is the explicit list of “open endpoints” allowed without auth (and why)?" },

{ id:"H0007", p:"P01", q:"What is the explicit list of “state-changing endpoints” that must be fail-closed and ledgered?" },

{ id:"H0008", p:"P01", q:"What is the maximum allowable payload size and maximum parse time, and how are both enforced?" },

{ id:"H0009", p:"P01", q:"What is the strategy for clock/time (UTC-only?) across Worker, DO, D1, and reports?" },

{ id:"H0010", p:"P01", q:"What is the deterministic ordering rule when multiple events share the same timestamp?" },

{ id:"H0011", p:"P01", q:"How do you guarantee the same behavior between local tests and Workers runtime (compat date, nodejs_compat, etc.)?" },

{ id:"H0012", p:"P01", q:"What is the explicit definition of “fail-closed” for each failure class (parse, db, do, r2, upstream)?" },

{ id:"H0013", p:"P01", q:"What is the “minimum viable evidence packet” and what fields must be included to be admissible?" },

{ id:"H0014", p:"P01", q:"What data must never enter logs/ledger, even redacted (forbidden categories)?" },

{ id:"H0015", p:"P01", q:"What is the single approved error envelope schema including stable error codes (not messages)?" },

{ id:"H0016", p:"P01", q:"What is the canonical list of error codes for auth failures vs misconfig vs upstream outages?" },

{ id:"H0017", p:"P01", q:"How do you ensure every response includes request_id, including early exits (OPTIONS, 401, 500)?" },

{ id:"H0018", p:"P01", q:"What is the plan for schema evolution of ledger rows without breaking admin/UI/export?" },

{ id:"H0019", p:"P01", q:"What is the plan for backwards-compat clients sending older payload shapes?" },

{ id:"H0020", p:"P01", q:"What is the “minimum unit of truth” for ledger: one row per user action, or per internal step, or both?" },

{ id:"H0021", p:"P01", q:"What is the model for correlating multiple ledger entries into one ‘operation’ (operation_id)?" },

{ id:"H0022", p:"P01", q:"What is the escalation policy when invariants fail (stop-line): who/what halts deploy?" },

{ id:"H0023", p:"P01", q:"What is the deterministic naming scheme for artifacts (zip, manifest) across runs?" },

{ id:"H0024", p:"P01", q:"What are the forbidden code patterns (eval/codegen) and how are they gated pre-deploy?" },

{ id:"H0025", p:"P01", q:"What is the single place where the system declares ‘done’ for a phase and seals it?" },



// P02 Auth & Identity (H0026–H0050)

{ id:"H0026", p:"P02", q:"Which header is canonical (one), and which aliases are supported (and for how long)?" },

{ id:"H0027", p:"P02", q:"Is auth required for GET requests that reveal metadata (counts, status, admin snapshots)?" },

{ id:"H0028", p:"P02", q:"How do you prevent auth bypass via alternate paths, trailing slashes, or query variations?" },

{ id:"H0029", p:"P02", q:"How do you handle multiple keys (rotation): dual-accept window, ordering, and audit logging?" },

{ id:"H0030", p:"P02", q:"What is the failure behavior when PHOENIX_MASTER_KEY is missing: 500 vs 503 vs 401?" },

{ id:"H0031", p:"P02", q:"How do you ensure OPTIONS preflight is never authenticated and never writes to ledger?" },

{ id:"H0032", p:"P02", q:"What is the canonical origin allowlist var (singular/plural) and do tests enforce it?" },

{ id:"H0033", p:"P02", q:"What are the exact CORS response headers and are they identical across all endpoints?" },

{ id:"H0034", p:"P02", q:"Do you allow wildcard origins? If yes, under what environments only?" },

{ id:"H0035", p:"P02", q:"What is the plan for CSRF protection for admin UI in a browser context?" },

{ id:"H0036", p:"P02", q:"Is admin protected by same key as execute, or separate admin key?" },

{ id:"H0037", p:"P02", q:"How is auth failure rate tracked and throttled to prevent brute force?" },

{ id:"H0038", p:"P02", q:"What data about auth failures is logged (and what is explicitly not logged)?" },

{ id:"H0039", p:"P02", q:"How do you handle whitespace, casing, and duplicate headers for the auth header?" },

{ id:"H0040", p:"P02", q:"How do you handle ‘Authorization: Bearer’ clients—explicitly reject or accept?" },

{ id:"H0041", p:"P02", q:"What is the auth policy for internal endpoints like /verifychain and /health?" },

{ id:"H0042", p:"P02", q:"How do you prevent key leakage through referrers, query strings, or error pages?" },

{ id:"H0043", p:"P02", q:"Do you enforce HTTPS-only assumptions and reject non-TLS forwarded headers?" },

{ id:"H0044", p:"P02", q:"Do you validate content-type strictly (exact match) or allow charset variants?" },

{ id:"H0045", p:"P02", q:"What is the policy for replay attacks: nonce, timestamp, or idempotency-only?" },

{ id:"H0046", p:"P02", q:"How do you prove that an authenticated request actually reached DO and wrote?" },

{ id:"H0047", p:"P02", q:"How do you handle compromised key: immediate revoke mechanism and blast radius?" },

{ id:"H0048", p:"P02", q:"What is the plan for per-client keys (multi-tenant) vs single sovereign key?" },

{ id:"H0049", p:"P02", q:"How do you ensure tests fail if any endpoint returns 500 for auth-negative cases?" },

{ id:"H0050", p:"P02", q:"What is the definitive auth contract doc and how is it auto-verified in CI?" },



// P03 Input Validation & Runtime Bans (H0051–H0075)

{ id:"H0051", p:"P03", q:"Which validation library is Workers-safe (no eval/codegen), and is it proven by a deploy smoke?" },

{ id:"H0052", p:"P03", q:"Do you forbid AJV compile at runtime explicitly (and how do you detect accidental reintroduction)?" },

{ id:"H0053", p:"P03", q:"What is the maximum depth/size for JSON to prevent parser bombs?" },

{ id:"H0054", p:"P03", q:"Do you reject unknown fields (strict mode) or allow pass-through (and how is it logged)?" },

{ id:"H0055", p:"P03", q:"How do you validate arrays: max length, item constraints, and type coercion rules?" },

{ id:"H0056", p:"P03", q:"How do you handle unicode normalization to prevent lookalike key attacks?" },

{ id:"H0057", p:"P03", q:"How do you handle numeric fields: NaN, Infinity, strings, and rounding?" },

{ id:"H0058", p:"P03", q:"How do you validate enums: case-insensitive? strict? migration path?" },

{ id:"H0059", p:"P03", q:"How do you enforce schema_version presence and reject unsupported versions deterministically?" },

{ id:"H0060", p:"P03", q:"What is the exact error response for validation failure (code, fields, path list)?" },

{ id:"H0061", p:"P03", q:"Do you validate Content-Length and reject mismatch or missing?" },

{ id:"H0062", p:"P03", q:"How do you prevent JSON injection in logs/admin rendering (escape policy)?" },

{ id:"H0063", p:"P03", q:"How do you prevent prototype pollution via __proto__/constructor keys?" },

{ id:"H0064", p:"P03", q:"Do you support multipart/form-data anywhere—explicitly reject?" },

{ id:"H0065", p:"P03", q:"What is the policy for extremely large strings (truncate vs reject), and is it consistent?" },

{ id:"H0066", p:"P03", q:"How do you validate timestamps provided by clients (or are they forbidden)?" },

{ id:"H0067", p:"P03", q:"How do you validate actor identity field to prevent log forging?" },

{ id:"H0068", p:"P03", q:"Is payload checksum computed server-side only, or do you accept client-provided checksums?" },

{ id:"H0069", p:"P03", q:"Do you enforce deterministic key ordering before hashing to avoid hash drift?" },

{ id:"H0070", p:"P03", q:"How do you handle partial validation: fail entire batch vs accept valid subset?" },

{ id:"H0071", p:"P03", q:"Do you have a ‘dry_run’ that guarantees zero writes (Worker + DO + DB)?" },

{ id:"H0072", p:"P03", q:"How do you ensure ‘dry_run’ cannot be bypassed by nested flags or alternate endpoints?" },

{ id:"H0073", p:"P03", q:"How do you validate that responses never contain secret substrings (response scrubber)?" },

{ id:"H0074", p:"P03", q:"Do you validate that error messages are stable (no dynamic details leaking internals)?" },

{ id:"H0075", p:"P03", q:"What is the unit test set that proves all of the above for Workers runtime specifically?" },



// P04 Ledger Integrity & Hashchain (H0076–H0100)

{ id:"H0076", p:"P04", q:"What is the canonical hashchain algorithm (fields included, order, encoding)?" },

{ id:"H0077", p:"P04", q:"How do you handle hashchain when entries are deleted/retained/purged?" },

{ id:"H0078", p:"P04", q:"Is the chain global or partitioned (by actor, by day, by tenant)?" },

{ id:"H0079", p:"P04", q:"What prevents a write that skips chain linkage (missing previous hash)?" },

{ id:"H0080", p:"P04", q:"How do you detect and record chain forks or concurrent writes?" },

{ id:"H0081", p:"P04", q:"What is the policy for reorg: if a write fails mid-flight, how is ‘gap’ recorded?" },

{ id:"H0082", p:"P04", q:"What is the canonical ‘verifychain’ output format and does it include proof material?" },

{ id:"H0083", p:"P04", q:"How do you ensure verifychain itself cannot be DOSed by scanning full history?" },

{ id:"H0084", p:"P04", q:"Do you sign the chain root (HMAC or signature) or is it raw hashes only?" },

{ id:"H0085", p:"P04", q:"Where is the chain root checkpoint stored (D1 row, R2 manifest, DO state)?" },

{ id:"H0086", p:"P04", q:"How do you handle out-of-order arrival: late events that should be earlier in time?" },

{ id:"H0087", p:"P04", q:"Is ‘created_at’ generated server-side only, and is client time ignored?" },

{ id:"H0088", p:"P04", q:"Do you store request_id inside the ledger row always, even for failures?" },

{ id:"H0089", p:"P04", q:"What is the schema for ‘actor’ and is it trustworthy or advisory?" },

{ id:"H0090", p:"P04", q:"How do you prevent ledger bloat from repeated identical events (dedupe policy)?" },

{ id:"H0091", p:"P04", q:"What is idempotency scope: per endpoint, per actor, per payload hash, per day?" },

{ id:"H0092", p:"P04", q:"What is the replay window duration, and how do you store it efficiently?" },

{ id:"H0093", p:"P04", q:"What happens if D1 write succeeds but response fails (client retries)?" },

{ id:"H0094", p:"P04", q:"What happens if DO state updated but D1 insert fails (compensation)?" },

{ id:"H0095", p:"P04", q:"Do you have a ‘ledger invariants’ checker that runs continuously (drone)?" },

{ id:"H0096", p:"P04", q:"Can admin export prove immutability (include previous hash links)?" },

{ id:"H0097", p:"P04", q:"How do you handle migrations that add columns without breaking hashing of old rows?" },

{ id:"H0098", p:"P04", q:"How do you handle redactions: do you store redacted payload or separate sealed blob?" },

{ id:"H0099", p:"P04", q:"What is the evidence of ledger completeness (no missing IDs)?" },

{ id:"H0100", p:"P04", q:"What is the exact process to seal a ledger segment and prove it later?" },



// P05 Cloudflare DO Concurrency & State (H0101–H0125)

{ id:"H0101", p:"P05", q:"Is every state-changing path routed through the DO (single-writer), with direct writes forbidden?" },

{ id:"H0104", p:"P05", q:"How do you prevent DO deadlocks or long awaits blocking subsequent events?" },

{ id:"H0105", p:"P05", q:"What is the plan for DO exceptions: do they fail whole request or return partial state?" },

{ id:"H0106", p:"P05", q:"How do you handle DO re-instantiation and loss of in-memory caches safely?" },

{ id:"H0107", p:"P05", q:"How do you protect DO endpoints from being called directly without worker guard?" },

{ id:"H0108", p:"P05", q:"How do you handle DO rate limiting: per key, per actor, per object name?" },

{ id:"H0109", p:"P05", q:"What is the object naming scheme (GLOBAL vs tenant-based) and why?" },

{ id:"H0110", p:"P05", q:"How do you test DO behavior deterministically in CI (miniflare) and match prod?" },

{ id:"H0111", p:"P05", q:"What is the policy for DO storage growth and compaction?" },

{ id:"H0112", p:"P05", q:"How do you ensure DO never returns 200 if D1 insert failed (atomicity contract)?" },

{ id:"H0113", p:"P05", q:"How do you record DO internal steps to ledger without leaking secrets?" },

{ id:"H0114", p:"P05", q:"Do you have a DO health check separate from worker /health?" },

{ id:"H0115", p:"P05", q:"What is the DO upgrade/migration process across versions (rolling deploy safety)?" },

{ id:"H0116", p:"P05", q:"How do you prevent double-processing on retry at DO layer (idempotency in DO)?" },

{ id:"H0117", p:"P05", q:"What is the DO timeout budget and how is it enforced?" },

{ id:"H0118", p:"P05", q:"How do you handle partial batch writes: per-item ledger entries or single batch entry?" },

{ id:"H0119", p:"P05", q:"Do you maintain a monotonic sequence number in DO to support gap detection?" },

{ id:"H0120", p:"P05", q:"How do you ensure DO’s notion of ‘latest’ matches D1 ordering exactly?" },

{ id:"H0121", p:"P05", q:"What happens if DO storage write succeeds and D1 fails repeatedly (backoff + queue)?" },

{ id:"H0122", p:"P05", q:"How do you prevent long-running verifychain from starving normal ingest in DO?" },

{ id:"H0123", p:"P05", q:"How do you handle multi-region and DO location assumptions (consistency)?" },

{ id:"H0124", p:"P05", q:"Do you log DO exceptions with request_id and stable error codes?" },

{ id:"H0125", p:"P05", q:"What is the DO-specific stop-line trigger list (P0 conditions)?" },



// P06 D1 Database & Query Safety (H0126–H0150)

{ id:"H0126", p:"P06", q:"What is the exact D1 schema (tables/indexes) and is it checked into migrations, not runtime create?" },

{ id:"H0127", p:"P06", q:"Do you forbid CREATE TABLE at request time to avoid race conditions?" },

{ id:"H0128", p:"P06", q:"What is the D1 migration order, and how do you detect partial migrations?" },

{ id:"H0129", p:"P06", q:"What is the max rows returned per query across all endpoints (hard cap)?" },

{ id:"H0130", p:"P06", q:"Do you have a query allowlist (prepared statements only) to prevent injection?" },

{ id:"H0131", p:"P06", q:"How do you handle large exports without timing out (chunking strategy)?" },

{ id:"H0132", p:"P06", q:"What is the index strategy for admin filters and sorting (explain/query plan)?" },

{ id:"H0133", p:"P06", q:"How do you ensure pagination is stable under concurrent writes (cursor vs offset)?" },

{ id:"H0134", p:"P06", q:"Do you store JSON as text—how do you query it safely and efficiently?" },

{ id:"H0135", p:"P06", q:"What is the retention strategy in D1 (delete vs archive) and how is it triggered?" },

{ id:"H0136", p:"P06", q:"How do you prevent admin UI from accidentally loading entire tables?" },

{ id:"H0137", p:"P06", q:"How do you handle D1 transient failures (retry policy) without duplicating writes?" },

{ id:"H0138", p:"P06", q:"What is the D1 connection check behavior on startup (and on each request)?" },

{ id:"H0139", p:"P06", q:"Do you validate that all writes include request_id and actor columns?" },

{ id:"H0140", p:"P06", q:"How do you handle schema_version per row and filtering by version?" },

{ id:"H0141", p:"P06", q:"Do you maintain a separate table for idempotency keys/replay window?" },

{ id:"H0142", p:"P06", q:"How do you ensure deletions are ledgered (a deletion is an event)?" },

{ id:"H0143", p:"P06", q:"How do you prevent hot partitions (same actor spamming) from degrading D1?" },

{ id:"H0144", p:"P06", q:"What is the backfill strategy when adding new indexes to existing data?" },

{ id:"H0145", p:"P06", q:"Do you have a D1 audit query to detect anomalies (missing hashes, duplicates)?" },

{ id:"H0146", p:"P06", q:"How do you guarantee consistent ordering in exports (created_at,id)?" },

{ id:"H0147", p:"P06", q:"What is the policy for storing large payloads: inline in D1 vs R2 blob pointer?" },

{ id:"H0148", p:"P06", q:"How do you prevent user-controlled fields from becoming query parameters unsafely?" },

{ id:"H0149", p:"P06", q:"Do you have a D1 performance budget and a regression gate?" },

{ id:"H0150", p:"P06", q:"What is the D1 disaster recovery story (backup/export + restore drill)?" },



// P07 R2 Artifacts, Evidence Sealing, and Upload Semantics (H0151–H0175)

{ id:"H0151", p:"P07", q:"What is the exact artifact schema (manifest fields) and is it versioned?" },

{ id:"H0152", p:"P07", q:"How do you ensure zip SHA is computed on the final file and not an intermediate path?" },

{ id:"H0153", p:"P07", q:"How do you ensure zip creation cannot recurse into itself (mktemp discipline)?" },

{ id:"H0154", p:"P07", q:"How do you detect wrangler syntax differences for R2 upload (bucket/key vs flags) reliably?" },

{ id:"H0155", p:"P07", q:"What is the idempotency rule for artifact uploads (same sha => no duplicate ledger entry)?" },

{ id:"H0156", p:"P07", q:"How do you verify the uploaded artifact matches local sha (download head check)?" },

{ id:"H0157", p:"P07", q:"What is the naming convention for R2 keys to avoid collisions across runs?" },

{ id:"H0158", p:"P07", q:"What is the retention policy for artifacts in R2 (expiry, lifecycle)?" },

{ id:"H0159", p:"P07", q:"Do artifacts include environment metadata (staging/prod), commit SHA, and tool versions?" },

{ id:"H0160", p:"P07", q:"Do artifacts include negative test evidence (401/403 capture) and exact curl command used?" },

{ id:"H0161", p:"P07", q:"How do you prevent secrets from being accidentally included in the zip (denylist scan on zip contents)?" },

{ id:"H0162", p:"P07", q:"What is the evidence chain for R2 object creation (who/when/request_id)?" },

{ id:"H0163", p:"P07", q:"How do you handle partial upload failures (resume vs retry vs new key)?" },

{ id:"H0164", p:"P07", q:"Do you enforce a max artifact size (zip cap) and fail closed if exceeded?" },

{ id:"H0165", p:"P07", q:"What is the policy for including logs: how many lines, what redaction, what time window?" },

{ id:"H0166", p:"P07", q:"How do you ensure artifact creation happens after deploy and tests, never before?" },

{ id:"H0167", p:"P07", q:"How do you record artifact sha into ledger and ensure it matches manifest sha?" },

{ id:"H0168", p:"P07", q:"What is the fallback if R2 is down—does pipeline halt (stop-line)?" },

{ id:"H0169", p:"P07", q:"How do you prevent bucket mis-targeting (staging artifact going to prod bucket)?" },

{ id:"H0170", p:"P07", q:"Do you sign manifests or otherwise tamper-proof them beyond hash links?" },

{ id:"H0171", p:"P07", q:"How do you handle tool version drift (wrangler changes) inside evidence packets?" },

{ id:"H0172", p:"P07", q:"Do you capture the full HTTP response body + status code correctly for all steps?" },

{ id:"H0173", p:"P07", q:"What is the ‘minimum set of files’ to include for forensic replay?" },

{ id:"H0174", p:"P07", q:"How do you ensure zips are reproducible (same inputs => same sha) if needed?" },

{ id:"H0175", p:"P07", q:"What is the artifact verification drill (restore + verifychain + compare)?" },



// P08 CI/CD, Tests, and Drift Gates (H0176–H0200)

{ id:"H0176", p:"P08", q:"Is there a single CI job that fails if any forbidden string appears anywhere (including built bundles)?" },

{ id:"H0177", p:"P08", q:"Do tests assert auth-negative returns ONLY 401/403 and never accept 500?" },

{ id:"H0178", p:"P08", q:"Do tests assert OPTIONS preflight succeeds without auth and with correct CORS headers?" },

{ id:"H0179", p:"P08", q:"Do tests assert ALLOWED_ORIGIN and ALLOWED_ORIGINS behavior matches production intent?" },

{ id:"H0180", p:"P08", q:"Do tests assert header aliases are accepted/rejected exactly as policy states?" },

{ id:"H0181", p:"P08", q:"Do tests assert /admin is guarded (or explicitly open) by an intentional toggle?" },

{ id:"H0182", p:"P08", q:"Do tests assert response envelopes include request_id everywhere?" },

{ id:"H0183", p:"P08", q:"Is there a ‘contract snapshot’ test that compares CANON config to deployed behavior?" },

{ id:"H0184", p:"P08", q:"Is there a deploy smoke test that runs against the actual staging URL after deploy?" },

{ id:"H0185", p:"P08", q:"Do you pin wrangler version in CI to avoid breaking CLI syntax drift?" },

{ id:"H0186", p:"P08", q:"Is there a gate that prevents prod deploy unless staging evidence packet is sealed?" },

{ id:"H0187", p:"P08", q:"Do you capture curl output correctly (body + status) in CI artifacts?" },

{ id:"H0188", p:"P08", q:"Do you test oversized payload rejection (413/400) and confirm no writes occurred?" },

{ id:"H0189", p:"P08", q:"Do you test idempotency (same request twice) and confirm only one ledger entry?" },

{ id:"H0190", p:"P08", q:"Do you test partial D1 failure simulation and confirm fail-closed behavior?" },

{ id:"H0191", p:"P08", q:"Do you test DO binding missing and confirm startup fails in a controlled way?" },

{ id:"H0192", p:"P08", q:"Is there a drift gate that fails if any hardcoded secrets exist in tests?" },

{ id:"H0193", p:"P08", q:"Is there a linter rule that forbids ‘Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *’ in prod code?" },

{ id:"H0194", p:"P08", q:"Do you verify codegen/eval bans by scanning bundle output for Function/eval usage?" },

{ id:"H0195", p:"P08", q:"Do you validate that compatibility_date changes are intentional and reviewed?" },

{ id:"H0196", p:"P08", q:"Do you ensure staging and prod use different secrets and cannot be cross-called accidentally?" },

{ id:"H0197", p:"P08", q:"Is there an invariant test that ledger hashchain verifies after any write?" },

{ id:"H0198", p:"P08", q:"Do you test CORS failure paths (bad origin) and ensure correct status and no leakage?" },

{ id:"H0199", p:"P08", q:"Do you have a CI gate that ensures docs are updated when routes/contracts change?" },

{ id:"H0200", p:"P08", q:"Do you have a CI ‘stop-line’ job that is the single authority to block merges?" },



// P09 Admin UI, Exposure, and Safety Defaults (H0201–H0225)

{ id:"H0201", p:"P09", q:"Does /admin ever render raw JSON without escaping (XSS risk)?" },

{ id:"H0202", p:"P09", q:"Does /admin fetch unbounded data by default (no limits)?" },

{ id:"H0203", p:"P09", q:"Is there a hard server cap even if client asks for limit=999999?" },

{ id:"H0204", p:"P09", q:"Does /admin include content-security-policy (CSP) with safe directives?" },

{ id:"H0205", p:"P09", q:"Does /admin include frame-ancestors to prevent clickjacking?" },

{ id:"H0206", p:"P09", q:"Does /admin include no-store headers always?" },

{ id:"H0207", p:"P09", q:"Can /admin reveal secrets via query params or reflected error messages?" },

{ id:"H0208", p:"P09", q:"Is there a safe ‘summary-only’ view to avoid rendering huge payloads?" },

{ id:"H0209", p:"P09", q:"Is there a safe export path with chunking and throttling to avoid timeouts?" },

{ id:"H0210", p:"P09", q:"Is the admin auth UX safe (never suggests placing keys in URLs)?" },

{ id:"H0211", p:"P09", q:"Does admin show request_id for every row and allow searching by it?" },

{ id:"H0212", p:"P09", q:"Does admin show chain verification status and last verified checkpoint?" },

{ id:"H0213", p:"P09", q:"Do you prevent admin from becoming an oracle for valid/invalid keys (timing differences)?" },

{ id:"H0214", p:"P09", q:"Is admin protected against accidental caching by edge or browser?" },

{ id:"H0215", p:"P09", q:"Does admin implement pagination with stable ordering (created_at,id)?" },

{ id:"H0216", p:"P09", q:"Are filters allowlisted (cannot filter on arbitrary columns)?" },

{ id:"H0217", p:"P09", q:"Does admin avoid showing PII by default (mask or omit)?" },

{ id:"H0218", p:"P09", q:"Is admin’s HTML free of blue (Gospel 444 compliance) enforced by a linter?" },

{ id:"H0219", p:"P09", q:"Is there an admin ‘panic switch’ to disable admin rendering quickly?" },

{ id:"H0220", p:"P09", q:"Does admin gracefully handle D1 errors without leaking stack traces?" },

{ id:"H0221", p:"P09", q:"Does admin have a health indicator that is backed by real checks (not static)?" },

{ id:"H0222", p:"P09", q:"Is there an admin audit log of admin accesses (actor, time, request_id)?" },

{ id:"H0223", p:"P09", q:"Does admin run in a separate route namespace to reduce accidental exposure?" },

{ id:"H0224", p:"P09", q:"Is there a hard cap on time range queries (e.g., last 7 days default)?" },

{ id:"H0225", p:"P09", q:"Is there a safe ‘download evidence packet’ flow that never reveals secrets?" },



// P10 Stage-4 Orchestrator & Script Fragility (H0226–H0250)

{ id:"H0226", p:"P10", q:"Is Stage-4 allowed to run from both PowerShell and WSL—if yes, do you have a single ‘truth path’ to avoid drift?" },

{ id:"H0227", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 detect OS/shell and fail closed if environment is unsupported?" },

{ id:"H0228", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 validate all required env vars are set before any action (preflight check)?" },

{ id:"H0229", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 enforce that deploy happens before migrations and tests (correct order)?" },

{ id:"H0230", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 validate wrangler auth is present (whoami) and correct account/zone?" },

{ id:"H0231", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 capture HTTP status and body correctly for all curl calls (no mixing)?" },

{ id:"H0232", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 require negative-auth test success (401/403) before continuing?" },

{ id:"H0233", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 confirm the deployed Worker is the expected version (header or /health version)?" },

{ id:"H0234", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 confirm migrations actually applied (schema check), not just ‘command succeeded’?" },

{ id:"H0235", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 validate D1 binding name matches code (DB vs LEDGER_DB etc.)?" },

{ id:"H0236", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 validate DO binding name/class matches wrangler config?" },

{ id:"H0237", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 handle wrangler CLI changes (command syntax detection) deterministically?" },

{ id:"H0238", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 use a unique run_id and include it in all artifacts and ledger writes?" },

{ id:"H0239", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 enforce retries only on retryable failures (network), never on validation/auth?" },

{ id:"H0240", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 limit retry count and include exponential backoff to prevent storms?" },

{ id:"H0242", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 prevent zipping secrets (scan tree before zip)?" },

{ id:"H0243", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 halt if it detects forbidden strings in repo (denylist) before deploy?" },

{ id:"H0244", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 verify R2 upload succeeded (head check) before ledger commit?" },

{ id:"H0245", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 ensure ledger commit is idempotent (same zip sha => no duplicate)?" },

{ id:"H0246", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 fail closed if any step returns non-expected status codes?" },

{ id:"H0247", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 generate a single summary report with links/refs to artifacts and request_ids?" },

{ id:"H0248", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 prove that the tested endpoint uses the same auth header as production clients?" },

{ id:"H0249", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 guard against accidentally running against prod URL during staging run?" },

{ id:"H0250", p:"P10", q:"Does Stage-4 produce an auditable ‘plan → confirm → execute’ transcript inside evidence?" },



// P11 Secrets, Leaks, Denylists, and Human Error (H0251–H0275)

{ id:"H0251", p:"P11", q:"Is there a denylist for master keys and known patterns, and does it run on commit + CI + artifact scan?" },

{ id:"H0252", p:"P11", q:"Do you scan for high-entropy strings (base64/hex) to catch unknown leaks?" },

{ id:"H0253", p:"P11", q:"Do you scan built bundles for secrets, not just source code?" },

{ id:"H0254", p:"P11", q:"Do you scrub secrets from exceptions (stack traces) before responding or logging?" },

{ id:"H0255", p:"P11", q:"Do you ensure secrets are only injected via Wrangler secrets and never via vars in git?" },

{ id:"H0256", p:"P11", q:"Do you have an SOP for rotating secrets with zero downtime and recorded evidence?" },

{ id:"H0257", p:"P11", q:"Do you have an SOP for handling a suspected compromise (invalidate, redeploy, audit)?" },

{ id:"H0258", p:"P11", q:"Do you enforce least privilege for API tokens used in CI (scoped tokens)?" },

{ id:"H0259", p:"P11", q:"Do you prevent secrets from being passed as CLI args (show in process list)?" },

{ id:"H0260", p:"P11", q:"Do you prevent secrets from being echoed in scripts (set -x dangers)?" },

{ id:"H0261", p:"P11", q:"Do you store any secret-derived hashes—are they vulnerable to offline guessing?" },

{ id:"H0262", p:"P11", q:"Is there a separate secret for staging vs prod and is it enforced by pipeline?" },

{ id:"H0263", p:"P11", q:"Do you ensure local dev uses dummy keys and cannot accidentally call prod?" },

{ id:"H0264", p:"P11", q:"Do you enforce ‘no secrets in chat logs’ policy (operator hygiene)?" },

{ id:"H0265", p:"P11", q:"Do you enforce that request bodies are never logged verbatim (only metadata)?" },

{ id:"H0266", p:"P11", q:"Do you ensure admin export does not include sensitive fields by default?" },

{ id:"H0267", p:"P11", q:"Do you ensure error responses never include env var names that hint internal structure?" },

{ id:"H0268", p:"P11", q:"Do you have a secret ‘format validator’ (length/pattern) to catch mis-set secrets early?" },

{ id:"H0269", p:"P11", q:"Do you have a ‘secret presence’ health check that does not reveal the secret itself?" },

{ id:"H0270", p:"P11", q:"Do you ensure rotation is captured in ledger as an event (who/when/why)?" },

{ id:"H0271", p:"P11", q:"Do you ensure old keys are invalidated after the dual-accept window ends automatically?" },

{ id:"H0272", p:"P11", q:"Do you have a kill-switch to disable accepts for all keys temporarily?" },

{ id:"H0273", p:"P11", q:"Do you have separate keys for read-only actions vs write actions (blast radius control)?" },

{ id:"H0274", p:"P11", q:"Do you have a policy for storing ‘operator identity’ for key usage auditing?" },

{ id:"H0275", p:"P11", q:"Do you have a tamper-evident record of all secret changes and approvals?" },



// P12 Rate Limiting, Abuse, and Resource Exhaustion (H0276–H0300)

{ id:"H0276", p:"P12", q:"What is the exact rate limit model (token bucket) and where does it live (DO vs Worker)?" },

{ id:"H0277", p:"P12", q:"Is rate limiting keyed by master key, IP, actor, or combined?" },

{ id:"H0278", p:"P12", q:"Do you allow bursts and communicate Retry-After correctly?" },

{ id:"H0279", p:"P12", q:"Do you protect admin endpoints with separate stricter limits?" },

{ id:"H0280", p:"P12", q:"Do you protect verifychain/export from being DOSed by expensive scans?" },

{ id:"H0281", p:"P12", q:"Do you cap max time range queries to prevent huge D1 scans?" },

{ id:"H0282", p:"P12", q:"Do you cap the number of records ingested per request (batch cap)?" },

{ id:"H0283", p:"P12", q:"Do you cap the total number of ingests per minute per key to protect D1 write limits?" },

{ id:"H0284", p:"P12", q:"Do you cap response sizes to prevent bandwidth spikes?" },

{ id:"H0285", p:"P12", q:"Do you cap admin HTML size and truncate large fields?" },

{ id:"H0286", p:"P12", q:"Do you cap recursion depth for nested objects to prevent compute blowups?" },

{ id:"H0287", p:"P12", q:"Do you have backpressure rules for transient D1 errors (queue vs reject)?" },

{ id:"H0288", p:"P12", q:"Do you implement circuit breakers for repeated upstream failures (AI APIs)?" },

{ id:"H0289", p:"P12", q:"Do you detect repeated bad requests and temporarily ban (abuse protection)?" },

{ id:"H0290", p:"P12", q:"Do you prevent timing attacks by making auth failure responses uniform?" },

{ id:"H0291", p:"P12", q:"Do you ensure large payload rejects do not parse entire body first (streaming guard)?" },

{ id:"H0292", p:"P12", q:"Do you have quotas for storage growth (D1 rows/day, R2 bytes/day)?" },

{ id:"H0293", p:"P12", q:"Do you have alert thresholds for approaching limits (budget burn)?" },

{ id:"H0294", p:"P12", q:"Do you prevent infinite retry loops in scripts and operators?" },

{ id:"H0295", p:"P12", q:"Do you test abuse scenarios in staging (burst, malformed, expensive endpoints)?" },

{ id:"H0296", p:"P12", q:"Do you have a policy for rejecting suspicious payloads (non-JSON, weird encodings)?" },

{ id:"H0297", p:"P12", q:"Do you ensure rate limit counters survive restarts (durable storage)?" },

{ id:"H0298", p:"P12", q:"Do you ensure rate limits are audited (who hit limit, when) without leaking secrets?" },

{ id:"H0299", p:"P12", q:"Do you have a manual override to raise limits temporarily with ledgered approval?" },

{ id:"H0300", p:"P12", q:"Do you have a stop-line condition tied to abuse metrics (auto halt)?" },



// P13 Multi-Model Routing, Magic Chat, and Output Integrity (H0301–H0325)

{ id:"H0301", p:"P13", q:"What is the routing decision schema stored in ledger (inputs, chosen model, reason, confidence)?" },

{ id:"H0302", p:"P13", q:"Do you store model outputs verbatim or summarized (and how do you handle privacy)?" },

{ id:"H0303", p:"P13", q:"How do you prevent prompt injection via user content from altering system governance instructions?" },

{ id:"H0304", p:"P13", q:"How do you guarantee “ledger-first context” when the model generates responses?" },

{ id:"H0305", p:"P13", q:"What is the verifier policy when models disagree (arbiter rules)?" },

{ id:"H0306", p:"P13", q:"Do you enforce output schemas for AI responses (structured) to prevent UI breakage?" },

{ id:"H0307", p:"P13", q:"Do you log which tools were used and which external calls were made (auditability)?" },

{ id:"H0308", p:"P13", q:"Do you cap token/context usage and record budget usage per request?" },

{ id:"H0309", p:"P13", q:"Do you have safe fallback behavior when AI providers are down (degraded mode)?" },

{ id:"H0310", p:"P13", q:"Do you store citations/links and ensure they’re valid (if used)?" },

{ id:"H0311", p:"P13", q:"Do you enforce “no secrets in prompts” at runtime (scrubber before sending to providers)?" },

{ id:"H0312", p:"P13", q:"Do you prevent hallucinated internal endpoints or secrets from being emitted to users?" },

{ id:"H0313", p:"P13", q:"What is the policy for storing conversations (retention, deletion) in ledger?" },

{ id:"H0314", p:"P13", q:"Do you distinguish human operator messages vs automated agent messages in ledger?" },

{ id:"H0315", p:"P13", q:"Do you record the exact prompt template version used for each AI call?" },

{ id:"H0316", p:"P13", q:"Do you record response latency per provider and use it in routing decisions?" },

{ id:"H0317", p:"P13", q:"Do you enforce provider-specific safety constraints and log enforcement decisions?" },

{ id:"H0318", p:"P13", q:"Do you verify that AI outputs do not violate Gospel 444 UI constraints (no blue tokens)?" },

{ id:"H0319", p:"P13", q:"Do you have tests that simulate adversarial user input (prompt injection) end-to-end?" },

{ id:"H0320", p:"P13", q:"Do you have a deterministic “explain routing” payload returned to UI safely?" },

{ id:"H0321", p:"P13", q:"Do you implement per-user or per-session context partitioning to avoid cross-contamination?" },

{ id:"H0322", p:"P13", q:"Do you have a policy for summarization drift (how summaries are corrected/ledgered)?" },

{ id:"H0323", p:"P13", q:"Do you have a policy for partial tool failures (one provider fails mid-response)?" },

{ id:"H0324", p:"P13", q:"Do you validate that stored AI outputs cannot break admin rendering (escape, truncate)?" },

{ id:"H0325", p:"P13", q:"Do you have an incident playbook for AI misbehavior (bad output) with stop-line triggers?" },



// P14 Memory, Ledger Search, and Retrieval Correctness (H0326–H0350)

{ id:"H0326", p:"P14", q:"What is the retrieval query contract (filters, limits) to prevent huge scans?" },

{ id:"H0327", p:"P14", q:"Do you implement deterministic pagination for search results (cursor)?" },

{ id:"H0328", p:"P14", q:"Do you store embeddings/vectors—if yes, where and with what privacy policy?" },

{ id:"H0329", p:"P14", q:"How do you prevent retrieval from returning sensitive data to unauthorized callers?" },

{ id:"H0330", p:"P14", q:"How do you handle memory correction: append correction entry vs edit (immutability)?" },

{ id:"H0331", p:"P14", q:"How do you tag confidence and provenance for retrieved facts?" },

{ id:"H0332", p:"P14", q:"How do you prevent “false memory” from being persisted via AI hallucination?" },

{ id:"H0333", p:"P14", q:"Do you have a whitelist of fields that can be persisted as memory (avoid sensitive data)?" },

{ id:"H0334", p:"P14", q:"Do you have a deletion/forget workflow and is it logged immutably?" },

{ id:"H0335", p:"P14", q:"Do you separate “ledger as evidence” vs “memory as convenience” in schema?" },

{ id:"H0336", p:"P14", q:"Do you handle staleness: how to revalidate facts over time?" },

{ id:"H0337", p:"P14", q:"Do you record retrieval decisions (why a memory was selected) for audit?" },

{ id:"H0338", p:"P14", q:"Do you enforce a maximum number of memory items injected into prompts?" },

{ id:"H0339", p:"P14", q:"Do you have a merge/dedupe strategy for near-duplicate memory entries?" },

{ id:"H0340", p:"P14", q:"Do you have an index strategy for memory queries (D1 indexes)?" },

{ id:"H0341", p:"P14", q:"Do you have a ‘memory poisoning’ test suite (adversarial entries)?" },

{ id:"H0342", p:"P14", q:"Do you include request_id and operation_id in all memory writes?" },

{ id:"H0343", p:"P14", q:"How do you partition memory by project/workspace to avoid mixing systems?" },

{ id:"H0344", p:"P14", q:"Do you have a “pinned canon” mechanism immune to summarization drift?" },

{ id:"H0345", p:"P14", q:"Do you have a safe export/import of memory for audits and backups?" },

{ id:"H0346", p:"P14", q:"Do you ensure search endpoints cannot be used to enumerate the entire ledger?" },

{ id:"H0347", p:"P14", q:"How do you prevent inconsistent results due to eventual consistency between DO and D1?" },

{ id:"H0348", p:"P14", q:"Do you have a policy for redacting memory entries retroactively without breaking evidence?" },

{ id:"H0349", p:"P14", q:"Do you store ‘negative knowledge’ (what is not true) and how is it used safely?" },

{ id:"H0350", p:"P14", q:"Do you have a validation drone that continuously samples memory correctness?" },



// P15 Config Canonicalization & Environment Split (H0351–H0375)

{ id:"H0351", p:"P15", q:"What is the canonical environment matrix (dev/stage/prod) and how do configs differ?" },

{ id:"H0352", p:"P15", q:"Do you enforce that staging URLs and prod URLs cannot be swapped accidentally (guardrails)?" },

{ id:"H0353", p:"P15", q:"Do you enforce distinct Cloudflare accounts/projects or namespaces per environment?" },

{ id:"H0354", p:"P15", q:"Is the CANON config itself signed/sealed and referenced by hash in ledger?" },

{ id:"H0355", p:"P15", q:"Do you validate that wrangler.toml matches the CANON config (no drift)?" },

{ id:"H0356", p:"P15", q:"Do you validate that secrets are present per environment (and not shared)?" },

{ id:"H0357", p:"P15", q:"Do you validate DO migrations are applied to correct environment only?" },

{ id:"H0358", p:"P15", q:"Do you validate D1 database binding points to correct DB id per environment?" },

{ id:"H0359", p:"P15", q:"Do you enforce compatibility_date pinned and changes audited?" },

{ id:"H0360", p:"P15", q:"Do you enforce feature flags by env (dangerous endpoints only in staging)?" },

{ id:"H0361", p:"P15", q:"Do you enforce logging verbosity by env (debug not in prod)?" },

{ id:"H0362", p:"P15", q:"Do you prevent CORS allow-all in prod while allowing convenience in dev?" },

{ id:"H0363", p:"P15", q:"Do you define a single ‘base URL’ source for Stage-4 to avoid copy/paste errors?" },

{ id:"H0364", p:"P15", q:"Do you validate that /health response contains environment marker (stage/prod)?" },

{ id:"H0365", p:"P15", q:"Do you enforce that admin is disabled or gated differently in prod?" },

{ id:"H0366", p:"P15", q:"Do you ensure tests run against staging deployment, not local simulation only?" },

{ id:"H0367", p:"P15", q:"Do you enforce that GitHub Actions secrets are not printed by any step?" },

{ id:"H0368", p:"P15", q:"Do you store toolchain versions (node, wrangler) in repo and use them in CI?" },

{ id:"H0369", p:"P15", q:"Do you maintain a change log of config changes separate from code changes?" },

{ id:"H0370", p:"P15", q:"Do you have a ‘rollback config’ mechanism if a config change breaks prod?" },

{ id:"H0371", p:"P15", q:"Do you validate that env var names used in code match those set in wrangler/CI?" },

{ id:"H0372", p:"P15", q:"Do you validate that header names used in tests match those used by Stage-4 and UI?" },

{ id:"H0373", p:"P15", q:"Do you enforce that all config is documented and docs are generated from canon?" },

{ id:"H0374", p:"P15", q:"Do you have a drift detector that compares deployed worker code hash to expected commit?" },

{ id:"H0375", p:"P15", q:"Do you have a ‘config stop-line’ if any canon mismatch is detected?" },



// P16 Endpoint Semantics & HTTP Correctness (H0376–H0400)

{ id:"H0376", p:"P16", q:"Are status codes consistent for all error types (401/403/404/405/413/429/500/503)?" },

{ id:"H0377", p:"P16", q:"Do you distinguish 401 (missing/invalid auth) vs 403 (valid auth but forbidden) intentionally?" },

{ id:"H0378", p:"P16", q:"Do you return JSON errors consistently even for 405/404 (content-type always JSON)?" },

{ id:"H0379", p:"P16", q:"Do you include ‘Vary: Origin’ for all CORS responses?" },

{ id:"H0380", p:"P16", q:"Do you include the correct Allow header for 405 responses?" },

{ id:"H0381", p:"P16", q:"Do you avoid leaking implementation via server headers and stack traces?" },

{ id:"H0382", p:"P16", q:"Do you handle HEAD requests intentionally (reject or support)?" },

{ id:"H0383", p:"P16", q:"Do you normalize trailing slashes and case in paths to prevent bypass?" },

{ id:"H0384", p:"P16", q:"Do you enforce JSON parse for POST only, and reject body on GET appropriately?" },

{ id:"H0385", p:"P16", q:"Do you provide consistent response timing (avoid side-channel for key validity)?" },

{ id:"H0386", p:"P16", q:"Do you include correlation headers (request_id) as response headers as well as body?" },

{ id:"H0387", p:"P16", q:"Do you support gzip/br compression and are you sure it won’t break client parsing?" },

{ id:"H0388", p:"P16", q:"Do you cap response time and return 503 if budgets exceeded?" },

{ id:"H0389", p:"P16", q:"Do you validate and parse query params safely (types, max length)?" },

{ id:"H0390", p:"P16", q:"Do you enforce ‘no caching’ for any endpoints returning ledger truth?" },

{ id:"H0391", p:"P16", q:"Do you implement ETags anywhere—if so, do they risk serving stale truth?" },

{ id:"H0392", p:"P16", q:"Do you handle OPTIONS for non-CORS contexts safely (always 204)?" },

{ id:"H0393", p:"P16", q:"Do you ensure error bodies are small and not echoing invalid payloads?" },

{ id:"H0394", p:"P16", q:"Do you return machine-readable validation errors (paths) for client correction?" },

{ id:"H0395", p:"P16", q:"Do you ensure admin HTML never includes user-controlled HTML (escape policy)?" },

{ id:"H0396", p:"P16", q:"Do you set correct content-type for HTML vs JSON endpoints always?" },

{ id:"H0397", p:"P16", q:"Do you have a consistent ‘success’ schema including operation_id and request_id?" },

{ id:"H0398", p:"P16", q:"Do you test HTTP semantics under browsers (real preflight) not just node fetch?" },

{ id:"H0399", p:"P16", q:"Do you ensure all early returns include CORS headers when origin allowed?" },

{ id:"H0400", p:"P16", q:"Do you have a single HTTP middleware layer to avoid duplicate diverging logic?" },



// P17 Observability for Real Failures (H0401–H0425)

{ id:"H0401", p:"P17", q:"Do logs include request_id, phase, endpoint, outcome, and latency as structured JSON?" },

{ id:"H0402", p:"P17", q:"Do you record which environment handled the request (stage/prod) in logs and ledger?" },

{ id:"H0403", p:"P17", q:"Do you record rate limit decisions and remaining tokens without revealing secret identity?" },

{ id:"H0404", p:"P17", q:"Do you record D1 query durations and row counts for performance regression?" },

{ id:"H0405", p:"P17", q:"Do you record DO execution time and storage operations count for bottlenecks?" },

{ id:"H0406", p:"P17", q:"Do you have alert thresholds for sudden spikes in 401/403/429/5xx?" },

{ id:"H0407", p:"P17", q:"Do you have alert thresholds for verifychain failures (integrity breach)?" },

{ id:"H0408", p:"P17", q:"Do you have a mechanism to sample and store raw failing requests safely (redacted)?" },

{ id:"H0409", p:"P17", q:"Do you have an incident timeline generator that links request_ids, commits, and artifacts?" },

{ id:"H0410", p:"P17", q:"Do you track ‘deployment change correlation’ (errors after deploy) automatically?" },

{ id:"H0411", p:"P17", q:"Do you store a minimal ‘runbook hint’ in error responses (non-sensitive)?" },

{ id:"H0412", p:"P17", q:"Do you maintain a dashboard for ‘pipeline health’ (deploy, migrate, test, seal)?" },

{ id:"H0413", p:"P17", q:"Do you measure CORS preflight success rate and browser failures?" },

{ id:"H0414", p:"P17", q:"Do you measure admin UI load time and time-to-first-byte under realistic ledger sizes?" },

{ id:"H0415", p:"P17", q:"Do you track artifact upload failures and timeouts (R2 reliability)?" },

{ id:"H0416", p:"P17", q:"Do you have a ‘panic mode’ reducing functionality when error rates exceed budget?" },

{ id:"H0417", p:"P17", q:"Do you have a daily integrity scan drone that verifies chain roots against checkpoints?" },

{ id:"H0418", p:"P17", q:"Do you track storage growth and predict when limits will be hit?" },

{ id:"H0419", p:"P17", q:"Do you track cost per operation and enforce a cost budget gate?" },

{ id:"H0420", p:"P17", q:"Do you record third-party AI API outages and correlate them to routing decisions?" },

{ id:"H0421", p:"P17", q:"Do you test alert noise and have a policy for alert tuning (reduce false positives)?" },

{ id:"H0422", p:"P17", q:"Do you have a strict logging redaction test to ensure secrets never appear in logs?" },

{ id:"H0423", p:"P17", q:"Do you store ‘evidence references’ (R2 key, sha) in logs for easy retrieval?" },

{ id:"H0424", p:"P17", q:"Do you have an SLO/error-budget policy that triggers stop-line automatically?" },

{ id:"H0425", p:"P17", q:"Do you have a restore drill metric (time to restore + verify) measured routinely?" },



// P18 Roblox Integration Holes (H0426–H0450)

{ id:"H0426", p:"P18", q:"What is the Roblox inbound authentication model (token rotation, signature, or key)?" },

{ id:"H0427", p:"P18", q:"What is the Roblox payload schema and how is versioning handled?" },

{ id:"H0428", p:"P18", q:"How do you prevent Roblox events from poisoning ledger/memory (validation + allowlists)?" },

{ id:"H0429", p:"P18", q:"Do Roblox events route through the same DO, or a dedicated DO to isolate load?" },

{ id:"H0430", p:"P18", q:"What is the rate limiting for Roblox event spikes (game launches)?" },

{ id:"H0431", p:"P18", q:"What is the backpressure strategy to avoid dropping critical Roblox events?" },

{ id:"H0432", p:"P18", q:"Do you need per-game or per-experience partitioning (tenant isolation)?" },

{ id:"H0433", p:"P18", q:"How do you map Roblox user IDs to internal actor identity safely?" },

{ id:"H0434", p:"P18", q:"Do you store any child data/PII—what is the compliance posture?" },

{ id:"H0435", p:"P18", q:"Do you have a replay protection mechanism for Roblox (nonce/timestamp)?" },

{ id:"H0436", p:"P18", q:"What is the error contract Roblox clients will see and how do they handle retries?" },

{ id:"H0437", p:"P18", q:"Do you have a staging Roblox environment and test harness?" },

{ id:"H0438", p:"P18", q:"Do you have an observability dashboard dedicated to Roblox ingest health?" },

{ id:"H0439", p:"P18", q:"Do you have a Roblox-specific evidence packet format for audits?" },

{ id:"H0440", p:"P18", q:"Do you have a kill-switch to disable Roblox ingest without affecting core Magic Chat?" },

{ id:"H0441", p:"P18", q:"Do you have schema drift detection for Roblox payloads (unknown fields)?" },

{ id:"H0442", p:"P18", q:"Do you have a ‘Roblox event dedupe’ strategy (idempotency keys)?" },

{ id:"H0443", p:"P18", q:"Do you have performance tests simulating peak Roblox traffic?" },

{ id:"H0444", p:"P18", q:"Do you have a cost model for Roblox ingestion (storage/day, calls/day)?" },

{ id:"H0445", p:"P18", q:"Do you have a plan for partially invalid Roblox batches (drop vs partial accept)?" },

{ id:"H0446", p:"P18", q:"Do you have a safe “debug mode” for Roblox that cannot be enabled in prod?" },

{ id:"H0447", p:"P18", q:"Do you have a mapping from Roblox events to business KPIs in a query-safe way?" },

{ id:"H0448", p:"P18", q:"Do you have a rollback story if Roblox integration breaks Worker deploy?" },

{ id:"H0449", p:"P18", q:"Do you have a separation of secrets: Roblox key distinct from sovereign master key?" },

{ id:"H0450", p:"P18", q:"Do you have a formal partner handshake doc (what Roblox must send, how often, errors)?" },



// P19 Scaling, Multi-Tenant, and Future-Proofing (H0451–H0475)

{ id:"H0451", p:"P19", q:"Is the system single-tenant by design, or will it become multi-tenant—what is the migration path?" },

{ id:"H0452", p:"P19", q:"If multi-tenant, how are tenants isolated in DO naming, D1 schema, and R2 keys?" },

{ id:"H0453", p:"P19", q:"Do you have a per-tenant key scheme and rotation policy?" },

{ id:"H0454", p:"P19", q:"How do you prevent one tenant’s traffic from starving others (fairness)?" },

{ id:"H0455", p:"P19", q:"Do you have a sharding strategy for ledger if it grows beyond comfortable D1 limits?" },

{ id:"H0456", p:"P19", q:"Do you have an archive strategy (older data to R2) with proofs and retrieval?" },

{ id:"H0457", p:"P19", q:"Do you have a query API for archived data that is safe and capped?" },

{ id:"H0458", p:"P19", q:"Do you have a ‘schema registry’ mechanism to support many payload types cleanly?" },

{ id:"H0459", p:"P19", q:"Do you have a plan for indexing strategy changes as data volume grows?" },

{ id:"H0460", p:"P19", q:"Do you have a plan for migrating from offset pagination to cursor pagination without breaking clients?" },

{ id:"H0461", p:"P19", q:"Do you have test datasets representing 10x and 100x growth to validate admin UX?" },

{ id:"H0462", p:"P19", q:"Do you have performance budgets per endpoint and per operation (not just global)?" },

{ id:"H0463", p:"P19", q:"Do you have a plan for eventual introduction of queues (if needed) while preserving ledger truth?" },

{ id:"H0464", p:"P19", q:"Do you have a plan for multi-region read paths while keeping write single-writer?" },

{ id:"H0465", p:"P19", q:"Do you have a method to snapshot chain roots periodically to speed verification?" },

{ id:"H0466", p:"P19", q:"Do you have a strategy for compressing payloads while keeping hashes stable?" },

{ id:"H0467", p:"P19", q:"Do you have a method to prove data completeness across shards/partitions?" },

{ id:"H0468", p:"P19", q:"Do you have a safe mechanism to evolve admin UI without exposing new data accidentally?" },

{ id:"H0469", p:"P19", q:"Do you have a roadmap for switching validators/libraries without runtime regression?" },

{ id:"H0470", p:"P19", q:"Do you have a plan for observability tool upgrades without breaking evidence format?" },

{ id:"H0471", p:"P19", q:"Do you have a disaster simulation for catastrophic provider outage (Cloudflare down)?" },

{ id:"H0472", p:"P19", q:"Do you have a cold-start performance plan as bundles grow (tree-shaking discipline)?" },

{ id:"H0473", p:"P19", q:"Do you have a governance process to approve new endpoints/features (stop-line review)?" },

{ id:"H0474", p:"P19", q:"Do you have a policy for long-term cryptographic agility (hash upgrade)?" },

{ id:"H0475", p:"P19", q:"Do you have a formal “scale readiness gate” with measurable pass criteria?" },

This is a massive, high-resolution audit framework. You have successfully mapped the "negative space"—the 500 specific failure modes that usually kill a system like Phoenix after deployment.

By explicitly defining these "Holes" (H0001–H0500), you have created a Devil's Advocate Engine. Instead of asking "What should we build?", this list asks "What have we forgotten to secure/ledger/verify?"

Here is the strategic summary of what you have just defined, and how we should likely proceed to close these holes:

The 20-Phase Gap Analysis
Foundations (P01-P03): You are prioritizing determinism over functionality. You aren't just asking if it works; you're asking if it works identically every time (time, order, validation).

The Ledger Core (P04-P06): This is the "Soul" of the system. You are treating the database not as a bucket, but as a cryptographic chain. The questions regarding D1 schema evolution (H0126) and DO concurrency (H0101) are critical for preventing data corruption in a serverless environment.

The Evidence Chain (P07-P08): This moves beyond "logging" to "forensics." The focus on artifacts, R2 storage, and proving that tests passed (H0186) changes the CI pipeline from a task runner into a notary public.

Security & Human Error (P09-P12): You are correctly assuming the operator (you/us) is a threat vector. The denylists, secret scrubbing, and admin safeguards are designed to protect the system from its creators.

AI & Logic (P13-P14): This acknowledges that LLMs are non-deterministic components in a deterministic system. The questions about routing decisions (H0301) and memory "poisoning" (H0341) are essential for maintaining truth in an AI wrapper.

Operations & Reality (P15-P20): These phases tackle the messy reality of production: drift, costs, Roblox integration quirks, and the inevitable panic when things break.

Immediate Execution Protocol

This plan moves specifically from Data Contract to Lua Implementation to Revenue Logic.

PHASE 1: THE FOUNDATION (Data Contract)
Define Goal: Confirm primary goal is Revenue Signaling (identifying Tiger students), not just analytics.

Define Payload Structure: Create the JSON schema for GameSession.

Field Definition (Identity): roblox_user_id (int), username (string).

Field Definition (Metrics): session_duration (seconds), deaths (int), wins (int).

Field Definition (Pedagogy): english_quiz_score (int) (if quizzes exist in-game).

Field Definition (Asset): current_skin (string) to track usage of "Mr. Bacon" / "Santa Admin".

Define Trigger Events: Start Session, End Session, Badge Awarded.

Versioning: Tag payload with v1.0 to handle future schema changes.

Privacy Check: Ensure no PII (Real Names) is sent from Roblox; map names in the Worker only.

Documentation: Create ROBLOX_API_CONTRACT.md in the Vault.

PHASE 2: ROBLOX STUDIO SETUP (The Environment)
Open Studio: Load "Survive a Brain Rot".

Enable HTTP: Go to Game Settings > Security > Enable Allow HTTP Requests.

Enable API Access: Go to Game Settings > Security > Enable Enable Studio Access to API Services.

Service Creation: Create a new ModuleScript named PhoenixUplink in ServerScriptService.

Secret Storage: Open Game Settings > Permissions > Add PHOENIX_KEY.

Environment Check: Write script to detect RunService:IsStudio() vs. Production.

Dependency Check: Ensure HttpService is required in the script.

Logger Setup: Create a local print function PhoenixLog() for Studio debugging.

Fail-Safe: Ensure script wraps calls in pcall (Protected Call) to prevent game crashes.

Commit Base: Save place to Roblox.

PHASE 3: THE LUA BRIDGE (Scripting)
Init Script: Write the basic HttpService:PostAsync function.

Header Config: Set Content-Type: application/json.

Auth Injection: Add x-deathstar-key header pulling from Secret Store.

Endpoint Config: Set url to https://phoenix-iron-clad.mrmichaelhobbs1234.workers.dev/execute.

Payload Builder: Write function to construct the JSON table from player data.

JSON Encode: Use HttpService:JSONEncode() on the table.

Response Handling: Write logic to parse the Worker's response (200 OK).

Retry Logic: Implement exponential backoff if the Worker is busy (429).

Queue System: If Worker is down, cache events in memory to retry later.

Unit Test: Run a manual PostAsync from the Command Bar to verify connectivity.

PHASE 4: WORKER-SIDE INGEST (The Brain)
Route Def: Create POST /roblox-ingest route in src/index.js (or use /execute).

Auth Gate: Enforce x-deathstar-key check immediately.

Schema Validation: Use the Native/AJV logic to reject malformed Roblox payloads.

Deduplication: Check request_id to prevent double-counting sessions.

Rate Limiting: Apply specific limits for Roblox IP ranges if necessary.

Data Parsing: Extract roblox_user_id and metrics.

Timestamping: Assign server-side timestamp (UTC) to the event.

Log Ingest: console.log the raw event for "Drone" monitoring.

Response: Return {"status": "ACK"} to Roblox so it stops retrying.

Deploy: wrangler deploy the updated Worker.

PHASE 5: THE TIGER LOGIC (Business Rules)
Identify Logic: Define what constitutes a "Tiger Signal."

Rule 1 (Tenacity): deaths > 10 AND play_time > 20 mins = DETERMINATION.

Rule 2 (Mastery): wins > 1 = SKILL.
